### [1] [Quasi-periodic oscillations in Letelier black holes within Kalb-Ramond gravity embedded in a uniform external magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12533)
*Ahmad Al-Badawi,Shokhzod Jumaniyozov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kalb-Ramond引力下被弦云包围的Letelier黑洞周围带电测试粒子的动力学行为,黑洞浸没在沿对称轴的外部渐近均匀磁场中。通过精确磁场解推导了运动方程、有效势、圆轨道参数和本征频率,并利用相对论性进动模型对三个X射线双星系统的高频准周期振荡进行了贝叶斯统计分析,结果排除了其中两个系统的史瓦西模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典史瓦西黑洞模型可能无法充分描述实际天体物理观测中的某些现象。需要研究更复杂的黑洞模型以解释高频准周期振荡(QPOs)等观测现象,特别是考虑弦云效应、Lorentz对称性破缺(Kalb-Ramond引力)以及外部磁场共同作用下的带电粒子动力学。这些修正效应可能为理解X射线双星系统的观测数据提供更好的理论框架。

Method: 1) 建立Kalb-Ramond引力下Letelier黑洞(被弦云包围)在外部均匀磁场中的时空几何模型,该几何仅通过弦云参数和KR参数的单一组合依赖于两者;2) 由于时空非Ricci平坦,推导出由高斯超几何函数闭式表达的正则无源磁场解(而非Wald势);3) 利用精确磁场解推导运动方程、有效势、圆轨道、最内稳定圆轨道(ISCO)以及轨道、径向和垂直本征频率的闭式表达,并通过数值积分验证;4) 在相对论性进动模型框架下,对三个X射线双星系统(GRO J1655-40、XTE J1550-564和GRS 1915+105)的双峰高频QPOs进行马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛(MCMC)分析,涵盖四个嵌套模型;5) 使用嵌套采样计算贝叶斯证据并对Larmor和反Larmor两种磁模式的后验概率进行加权。

Result: 1) 获得了磁场的闭式解,其在史瓦西极限下退化为Wald解;2) 推导出完整的带电粒子动力学参数:有效势、圆轨道、ISCO位置、代表性逃逸/捕获/卷曲轨迹以及三种本征频率的闭式表达;3) 所有频率表达式通过完整运动方程的直接数值积分得到交叉验证;4) 贝叶斯统计分析显示:对于GRO J1655-40和XTE J1550-564两个系统,史瓦西模型被排除,Akaike信息准则差异约为110,表明修正模型显著优于标准史瓦西模型;5) 成功对三个X射线双星系统的高频QPOs数据进行了拟合,获得了不同模型的后验分布和贝叶斯证据。

Conclusion: 本研究在Kalb-Ramond引力框架下建立了被弦云包围的Letelier黑洞磁层中带电粒子动力学的完整理论体系,获得了精确的磁场解和运动学参数的闭式表达。通过对三个X射线双星系统高频QPOs的严格贝叶斯分析,证明了对于GRO J1655-40和XTE J1550-564,包含弦云和KR参数修正的模型比经典史瓦西模型具有显著的统计优势(ΔAICc≈110)。这表明弦云效应和Lorentz对称性破缺可能在实际天体物理系统中发挥作用,为检验量子引力效应和修正引力理论提供了观测约束。研究还揭示了弦云参数和KR参数可以相互补偿的特性,当两者相等时可恢复史瓦西度规。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of charged test particles around a Letelier black hole, i.e. a black hole surrounded by a cloud of strings (CoS), in Kalb-Ramond (KR) gravity, immersed in an external, asymptotically uniform magnetic field aligned with the symmetry axis. The geometry depends on the CoS parameter and on the Lorentz-violating KR parameter only through a single combination of the two, so that the two effects can compensate each other and the Schwarzschild metric is recovered when both parameters are equal. Because the spacetime is not Ricci flat, the Wald potential is not a solution of the Maxwell equations; we show that the regular source-free magnetic test field is given in closed form by a Gauss hypergeometric function of the radial coordinate, which reduces to the Wald solution in the Schwarzschild limit. Using this exact field we derive the equations of motion, the effective potential, the circular orbits, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO), representative escaping, captured and curled trajectories, and the orbital, radial and vertical epicyclic frequencies in closed form; all frequency expressions are cross-checked against direct numerical integration of the full equations of motion. Within the relativistic precession model, we perform a Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) analysis of the twin-peak high-frequency quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs) of GRO J1655-40, XTE J1550-564 and GRS 1915+105 for four nested models, complemented by nested sampling to compute Bayesian evidences and to weight the two (Larmor and anti-Larmor) magnetic modes of the posterior. The Schwarzschild model is excluded for GRO J1655-40 and XTE J1550-564, with a difference in the Akaike information criterion of about 110.

</details>


### [2] [Asymptotic AEther Misalignment and Universal-Horizon Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12559)
*Walter Arata,Moustafa Ismail,Stefano Liberati,David Mattingly,Luke Martin,Giulio Neri*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文解决了Einstein-aether理论中渐近反德西特黑洞在存在以太场渐近倾斜时无法恢复黑洞力学第一定律的问题,通过协变相空间形式论识别边界源及其共轭响应,建立了包含度规面积贡献和不可约以太能量流的广义第一定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Einstein-aether理论中,以太场定义了优选时间方向,但当以太场相对于无穷远处的静止时间存在渐近倾斜时,传统的黑洞力学第一定律无法适用于渐近反德西特黑洞。这一理论困境阻碍了在具有优选时间方向的理论中检验黑洞热力学,需要找到新的理论框架来恢复第一定律。

Method: 采用协变相空间形式论(covariant phase-space formalism)分析静态球对称精确解族。将以太场的渐近倾斜识别为边界源,将以太场的衰减部分识别为其共轭响应。通过固定源或响应(或建立二者之间的关系)来定义产生时间平移的能量,外部改变边界数据则在第一定律中引入功。通过Legendre变换关联固定源和固定响应所得到的不同能量形式。

Result: 成功为存在以太场渐近倾斜的渐近反德西特黑洞建立了黑洞力学第一定律。在普适视界处,热量包含度规面积贡献和不可约的以太能量流两部分。获得的能量平衡关系成功分离了无穷远处的功和视界热量。证明了通过固定边界源或响应得到的能量通过Legendre变换相关联。

Conclusion: 本研究成功解决了Einstein-aether理论中带以太场渐近倾斜的反德西特黑洞的第一定律问题,揭示了物理温度通常不能从黑洞质量中面积变分的系数直接推断的原因。热量的广义形式包含面积项和以太能量流,而能量定义依赖于边界条件的选择,不同选择通过Legendre变换关联,为检验具有优选时间方向理论中的黑洞热力学提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Universal horizons trap even arbitrarily fast signals, providing a test of black-hole thermodynamics in theories with a preferred time direction. In Einstein--aether theory, this direction is defined by a dynamical field, the aether, that can remain non-aligned relative to stationary time at infinity. In particular, for asymptotically anti-de Sitter black holes, it was so far impossible to recover a first law of black-hole mechanics when such asymptotic tilt of the aether is present. Here, we resolve this issue for an exact family of static, spherically symmetric solutions. Using the covariant phase-space formalism, we identify the asymptotic tilt and the decaying part of the aether field as a boundary source and its conjugate response. Allowing both to vary independently obstructs the definition of an energy that generates time translations. Fixing either quantity, or imposing a relation between them, restores such an energy, while externally varying the boundary data introduces work into the first law. The energies obtained by fixing the source or its response are related by a Legendre transformation. At the universal horizon, heat includes both the metric area contribution and an irreducible aether energy flux. The resulting balance separates work at infinity from horizon heat and explains why the physical temperature cannot generally be inferred from the coefficient of an area variation in the black-hole mass.

</details>


### [3] [Dynamics of Reheating in Modified Loop Quantum Cosmologies: mLQCI-I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12639)
*Jared Fier,Rui Pan,Jamal Saeed,Anzhong Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了修正圈量子宇宙学中的再加热时期和从量子反弹到现在的总膨胀,利用标量振幅和谱指数的观测数据,分析了不同暴胀势(幂律平台势、Starobinsky势和多项式混沌势)对再加热参数和总膨胀的影响,发现再加热过程强烈依赖于势的形式,而总膨胀主要由LQC和mLQC-I的背景量子几何差异决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圈量子宇宙学(LQC)中的再加热时期和总膨胀特性对于连接早期宇宙量子效应与观测宇宙学至关重要,但不同暴胀势模型和量子几何背景如何影响这些关键参数尚不清楚。本研究旨在利用当前观测约束系统地分析修正圈量子宇宙学中再加热和总膨胀对势函数形式及归一化的敏感性,以区分模型依赖效应和量子几何本质特征。

Method: 研究采用动能主导初始条件,结合标量振幅和谱指数的观测值独立确定暴胀势的归一化。分析了三类代表性势函数:幂律平台势、Starobinsky势和多项式混沌势。通过数值计算评估量子反弹、暴胀演化和再加热过程,计算关键参数包括再加热e-fold数、再加热温度以及从反弹到现在的总膨胀数N。系统比较了标准LQC与修正版本mLQC-I在不同势函数下的表现差异。

Result: 量子反弹阶段对势函数归一化极不敏感,但暴胀和再加热演化显示出明显依赖性。再加热e-fold数和再加热温度强烈依赖于势函数的具体形式,在多项式情况下还依赖于所选分支。总膨胀在所考虑的参数范围内满足保守界N > 129,并保持接近LQC特征尺度。mLQC-I与LQC在总膨胀上的差异对所有三种势函数都表现出显著的稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究表明再加热过程具有强烈的模型依赖性,受暴胀势函数形式的显著影响,而总膨胀的小幅系统偏移主要源于LQC和mLQC-I不同背景量子几何的本质差异,而非势函数选择。这揭示了量子几何效应与暴胀动力学在早期宇宙演化中的不同作用:量子反弹和总膨胀主要由量子引力修正决定,而再加热细节则由具体的粒子物理模型主导。结果为通过观测区分量子引力效应和暴胀模型提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study the reheating epoch and total bounce-to-present expansion in modified loop quantum cosmology using current observational determinations of the scalar amplitude and spectral index. We consider the power-law plateau potential, whose normalization is determined independently from the observed values of the scalar amplitude and spectral index. We find that, for the kinetic-energy-dominated initial conditions, the quantum-bounce regime is remarkably insensitive to this normalization, while the subsequent inflationary and reheating evolution shows a more appreciable dependence. We then investigate the sensitivity of the results to the inflationary potential by considering the Starobinsky and polynomial chaotic potentials. The reheating e-fold number and reheating temperature are found to depend strongly on both the form of the potential and, in the polynomial case, the branch selected. In contrast, the total bounce-to-present expansion satisfies the conservative bound N > 129 over the ranges considered here and remains close to the characteristic scale found in LQC. Moreover, the difference between mLQC-I and LQC is remarkably stable for all three potentials. These results suggest that reheating is strongly model dependent, whereas the small systematic shift in the total expansion is primarily associated with the different background quantum geometries of LQC and mLQC-I.

</details>


### [4] [Quantum geometric inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13019)
*Nephtalí Eliceo Martínez Pérez,Cupatitzio Ramírez Romero*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对几何暴胀(Geometric Inflation)模型进行Wheeler-DeWitt量子化研究,通过构建有效一维FRW作用量并应用Hamiltonian形式化方法,研究了无需暴胀子场的宇宙早期暴胀机制的量子行为,特别针对辐射情况建立了分数阶微分方程系统并构造了动量空间波包解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何暴胀模型通过在Einstein-Hilbert作用量中引入无限塔曲率不变量来实现暴胀,无需传统的暴胀子场。为了理解该模型的量子特性和早期宇宙的量子行为,需要对其进行正则量子化处理,这促使研究者采用Wheeler-DeWitt量子化方法来研究minisuperspace几何暴胀模型的量子动力学。

Method: 研究采用了以下方法:(1)构建有效的一维FRW作用量;(2)采用Ostrogradsky方法和Dirac约束系统算法进行Hamiltonian形式化处理;(3)基于Schutz形式论处理理想正压流体的Lagrangian和Hamiltonian;(4)建立Wheeler-DeWitt方程并进行解析和数值求解;(5)对于辐射情况,使用Caputo分数阶导数算子处理分数阶微分方程组;(6)在动量空间构造波包,以流体变量作为外部时间参数。

Result: 研究成功建立了几何暴胀模型的Wheeler-DeWitt量子描述框架。对于辐射主导情况,导出了分数阶微分方程系统,并获得了解析和数值解。通过在动量空间构造波包,实现了用流体变量作为外部时间参数的量子态描述,为理解该模型的量子行为提供了具体的数学工具和物理图像。

Conclusion: 本研究成功实现了几何暴胀模型的Wheeler-DeWitt量子化,证明了无需暴胀子场的暴胀机制在量子层面的自洽性。研究建立了完整的Hamiltonian形式化框架,特别是通过分数阶微分方程描述了辐射情况下的量子动力学。波包构造方法为进一步研究量子宇宙学提供了有效途径,展示了几何暴胀作为替代传统暴胀理论的可行性。

Abstract: Geometric Inflation adds an infinite tower of curvature invariants to the Einstein-Hilbert action while preserving certain key features such as leading to second order modified Friedmann equations for FRW backgrounds. Sourcing them with a perfect barotropic fluid can easily produce a slow-roll inflationary regime, no inflaton field is required. In this work, we perform the Wheeler-DeWitt quantization of minisuperspace Geometric Inflation models. First, we construct an effective 1D FRW action and perform the Hamiltonian formulation using both Ostrogradsky's approach and Dirac's algorithm for constrained systems. The Lagrangian and Hamiltonian treatment of the perfect fluid is based on Schutz's formalism. Second, we postulate the Wheeler-DeWitt equation and investigate its solutions both analytically and numerically. For radiation we are led to a system of fractional differential equations for which we use Caputo fractional derivative operator. We construct wave packets in momentum space, with the fluid's variable acting as external time parameter.

</details>


### [5] [Causality, Redshift, and Matching-Scale Cancellation for Reflective Black-Hole Horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13109)
*Masahiro Kaminaga*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究施瓦西黑洞散射中量子视界模型的因果边界响应问题，证明了经典Hardy空间条件排除精确玻尔兹曼模在整个正频轴上的存在性，构造了保持有限频带模的因果完备化方案，并推导出低频外相位展开式，揭示了几何相位抵消与色散余项的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子视界模型中使用的玻尔兹曼通量反射率本身并不能定义因果黑洞边界响应。需要解决两个核心问题：一是如何在满足因果性约束的前提下构造与玻尔兹曼模相容的反射振幅；二是如何理解不同完备化方案在低频极限下的相位行为及其物理意义，特别是匹配距离相关的相位依赖性及其几何起源。

Method: 1) 利用Hardy空间理论证明经典因果性条件与精确玻尔兹曼模的不相容性；2) 构造两类因果完备化方案：精确频带完备化和光滑交叉完备化，保持指定有限频带上的玻尔兹曼模；3) 对具有固定轮廓和共同玻尔兹曼斜率的完备化类别，推导低频外相位的渐近展开式（对数项和线性项）；4) 分析当固定本征系截断红移到匹配面、局域温度取霍金温度的Tolman红移时的相位抵消机制；5) 在轴对称施瓦西算例中数值验证线性延迟差修正对腔因子的影响；6) 引入被动量子完备化提供吸收-噪声关系并检验渐近输出的一致性。

Result: 1) 证明了任何非零有界因果反射振幅在固定匹配面上不能在整个正频轴上精确具有玻尔兹曼模；2) 成功构造了在任意规定有限频带上保持玻尔兹曼模的因果完备化；3) 得到低频外相位展开式，其中对数系数对该完备化类别是共同的，而线性常数是完备化的显式泛函；4) 发现外相位抵消了传播相位对匹配距离的主导对数依赖，几何余项为本征距离的二次项，色散余项依赖于具体完备化；5) 在轴对称施瓦西算例中，考虑预测的线性延迟差后，两个截断之间的最大相对腔因子差从约21%降至0.11%；6) 被动量子完备化确认了渐近输出的一致性。

Conclusion: 本研究阐明了量子视界模型中因果性约束与玻尔兹曼反射率之间的基本张力，表明精确玻尔兹曼模与因果性在全频域不相容，但可通过适当的完备化方案在物理相关的有限频带上实现相容。低频相位展开揭示了匹配面位置的对数依赖性可通过适当的温度红移选择得到抵消，剩余的几何和色散项反映了不同完备化方案的物理差异。这是对特定响应族的低能比较，而非精确的匹配不变性或单纯由因果性固定的相位关系。数值算例验证了理论预测的有效性，被动量子完备化框架为黑洞边界的量子响应提供了自洽的物理图像。

Abstract: The Boltzmann flux reflectivity used in quantum-horizon models does not by itself define a causal black-hole boundary response. We study two related questions for stationary Schwarzschild scattering. First, a classical Hardy-space condition excludes an exact Boltzmann modulus on the entire positive frequency axis for any nonzero bounded causal reflection amplitude at a fixed matching plane. We construct causal completions that preserve the modulus on any prescribed finite band. Second, we prove a low-frequency outer-phase expansion for a class of completions with fixed profiles and a common Boltzmann slope, including an exact-band completion and a smooth crossover completion. The logarithmic coefficient is common to this class, while the linear constant is an explicit functional of the completion. When the fixed proper-frame cutoff is redshifted to the matching surface and the local temperature is the Tolman redshift of the Hawking temperature, the outer phase cancels the leading logarithmic dependence of the propagation phase on the matching distance. The geometric remainder is quadratic in the proper distance, while a separate dispersive remainder depends on the completion. This is a low-energy comparison of specified response families, not an exact matching invariance or a phase fixed by causality alone. In an axial Schwarzschild example at a fixed matching plane, accounting for the predicted linear-delay difference reduces the maximum relative cavity-factor difference between two cutoffs from about $21\%$ to $0.11\%$ on the sampled frequency band. A passive quantum completion supplies the associated absorption-noise relation and checks the consistency of the asymptotic output.

</details>


### [6] [Binary Ellis-Bronnikov wormholes in 3+1 numerical relativity: mergers, fly-bys and gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13206)
*Nikita M. Shirokov,Ilya Nachevsky*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次对虫洞双星系统进行了3D数值相对论演化模拟,研究了由幽灵标量场支撑的Ellis-Bronnikov排水孔对的动力学行为,发现喉部不稳定、相反电荷吸引合并、碰撞形成视界并收缩至史瓦西半径附近,且系统辐射引力波和负能量标量偶极子,导致双星无法形成稳定的旋近过程。

Conclusion: 本文成功构造了度规仿射Kalb-Ramond引力理论中的精确旋转黑洞解,该解推广了Kerr度规并包含由曲率耦合参数组合ε刻画的修正效应。解具有良好的几何性质(视界光滑性、渐近平直性),其物理可观测量(光子阴影、轨道动力学)可用于检验修正引力理论。水星观测数据对参数ε提供了10⁻¹¹量级的约束,但曲率耦合参数之间存在简并性,需要更多独立观测来完全确定理论参数空间。

Abstract: We construct an exact stationary axisymmetric vacuum configuration in metric affine Kalb-Ramond gravity with zero cosmological constant. Independent variation of the metric, connection, and antisymmetric field yields the complete field equations and the algebraic relation between the physical and auxiliary geometries. A Kerr auxiliary metric and a closed corotating vacuum two form of fixed positive norm determine the physical metric. The tensor kernel contains the horizon generator, and the metrics, independent connection, and vacuum field share a smooth extension across the nonextremal physical Killing horizon and the rotation axes at finite radius for unequal deformation factors. We obtain an explicit sufficient domain for a causal exterior and an asymptotically locally flat end at fixed nonpolar angles. The physical Hamiltonian yields the timelike and null equations, equatorial effective potentials, circular orbit properties, radial stability, and perihelion advance. Numerical photon evolution determines the critical curve on an orthonormal observer screen. Its size, shape, and centroid depend on $ε=ξ_2b^2/[2(1+ξ_1b^2)]$ at fixed normalized mass scale and spin, with Kerr recovered at $ε=0$. Geometric observations probe this combination and retain an exact degeneracy between the curvature couplings. A static equatorial comparison with Mercury gives a conditional sensitivity of $2.30\times10^{-11}$ for $|2ε|$ under the stated angular convention and mass setting.

Abstract: We report the first Monte Carlo simulation of quantum field theory on Euclidean de Sitter space, $S^4$. Rather than discretizing space, we use a spectral lattice based on spherical harmonics, keeping all symmetries exact at finite cutoff. For a massless $λφ^4$ theory, the zero-mode distribution reproduces the Starobinsky-Yokoyama equilibrium at weak coupling, and deviations closely follow the one-loop renormalization of $λ$. At strong bare coupling, the renormalized quartic coupling saturates near the ceiling of the one-loop renormalization-group flow. This saturation is the de Sitter counterpart of the triviality mechanism that bounds the Higgs mass in flat space. Our results confirm nonperturbatively that the infrared divergences of light scalars in de Sitter are correctly resummed by the stochastic formalism. More broadly, this work opens up de Sitter space to lattice Monte Carlo methods.

The gravitational on-shell action localizes entirely to the north and south poles of each black hole, with the two contributions determined by the holomorphic and anti-holomorphic topological string free energies, respectively, evaluated at the attractor values of the moduli. For single-center black hole saddles, this provides a proof of the OSV conjecture to all orders in the topological string coupling. The same localization formula also applies to all saddles that contribute to the helicity supertrace, such as multi-center black holes, which leads to a non-perturbative refinement of the conjecture.

Abstract: Gauged Linear Sigma Models (GLSMs) have long been a key tool in understanding Calabi-Yau compactifications of string theory. While descriptions of complete intersections of polynomial defining equations have long been understood in this context, a full treatment of non-polynomial complex structure deformations has been absent from the literature. Likewise, the somewhat more exotic generalized complete intersection Calabi-Yau construction has resisted a GLSM description. In this paper we resolve both of these omissions using completely standard GLSMs with polynomial superpotentials. The non-polynomial structure seen in the geometric constructions only arises in the associated GLSM upon restriction to the vacuum space of the theory. This feature enables the construction to avoid singularities in field space that could have arisen if non-polynomial structure were incorporated directly into the GLSM superpotential.

Restricting for simplicity to the case of a superfluid, we determine via effective field theory methods the corresponding low-frequency contribution to the imaginary part of the index of refraction. This scales as $ω^4$ and induces a nonanalyticity of the real part for $ω\rightarrow 0$. We also extend our result to large coherent electromagnetic fields, such as those of a laser, showing that the effect can receive strong nonlinear enhancements. Our result is a genuine quantum lower bound to the transparency of a medium.

Abstract: The S-matrix bootstrap restricts low-energy interactions through unitarity, analyticity and crossing symmetry. For effective field theory couplings, positivity bounds have a natural geometric formulation, the EFThedron, in which the allowed couplings form the classical positive moment problem. We show that this geometry extends to full nonlinear unitarity. Multiplying the amplitude by fractional powers of the energy turns its real part into a moment as well, so that unitarity becomes a bounded complex moment problem. Analyticity becomes a set of linear constraints requiring different kernels to reconstruct the same couplings, analogous to the null constraints imposed by crossing symmetry. In two dimensions the problem can be solved exactly, leading to Castillejo--Dalitz--Dyson amplitudes as extremal measures of the moment problem. In four dimensions we obtain closed-form and semi-analytic bounds for the massless EFThedron model and for massive scalar scattering. For massive scalars, the analyticity constraints improve on published dual bounds, reducing the gap to the primal region. By placing the real and imaginary parts of amplitudes on an equal footing, the Real EFThedron provides a powerful framework combining unitarity, analyticity and crossing, opening a new route to mapping the space of consistent quantum field theories.

Abstract: A tropical quantum field theory is an exactly solvable deformation limit of an ordinary QFT, governed by a non-linear partial differential equation. For the tropical sine-Gordon model, we compute the $β$-functions exactly, solve the renormalized theory both via a convergent expansion and numerically for all admissible couplings, and illustrate the complete renormalization group flow from the Gaussian to the IR fixed point. Abstract: We study open strings in a constant magnetic field from the viewpoint of universal canonical variables. Abstract: We discuss General Relativity and some of its modifications as effective field theories of the breaking of the affine general linear group using the coset approach and the Maurer-Cartan form. Interestingly, these operators do not appear perturbatively but emerge from the calculation, raising the question of what operators the $(0,2)$ elliptic genus actually counts. Abstract: We describe a family of two-dimensional metric deformations that preserve integrability. For Weyl-invariant seed matter theories, the auxiliary metric sector is described by a reduced gravitational potential. We show that the resulting framework preserves classical Lax integrability whenever the seed matter theory admits a Lax representation of its equations of motion on a generic curved two-dimensional spacetime. Finally, within the Lorentz-invariant family and the zweibein deformations of conformal matter theories considered here, we identify $T\bar{T}$ as the only deformation that can be interpreted as a field-dependent coordinate transformation.

Abstract: We investigate holographic Krylov complexity in bottom-up holographic QCD models describing the confined phase, using the recently proposed correspondence between the growth rate of Krylov complexity and the proper radial momentum of probes, such as particles and strings, falling in the bulk spacetime. As representative confining backgrounds, we consider two horizonless geometries, namely the dilaton-deformed AdS-soliton and the deformed thermal-AdS spacetime, both arising as solutions of an Einstein-dilaton gravity system. We find that the Krylov complexity associated with a massive particle exhibits qualitatively distinct behavior in these two backgrounds when analyzed in the Einstein frame. In the deformed AdS-soliton geometry, the complexity undergoes periodic oscillations, with both the oscillation period and amplitude depending on the parameters of the model. In contrast, in the deformed thermal-AdS geometry, the complexity grows exponentially at late times and does not display oscillatory behavior. We then investigate string dynamics in the string frame and find that the Krylov complexity exhibits oscillatory behavior in both confining backgrounds. This behavior is traced to the emergence of an additional turning point in the deep infrared of the string-frame geometry. Motivated by this observation, we further analyze massive-particle dynamics in the string frame, where we find qualitatively similar behavior in the two confining backgrounds. Our results demonstrate that the Krylov complexity of confined holographic systems depends sensitively on the choice of probe and frame, while also revealing a closer correspondence between the two confining geometries in the string-frame description.

Abstract: The $SU(2)$ Skyrme model admits exact solutions when coupled to General Relativity in four spacetime dimensions. Here, we demonstrate that relevant gravitational configurations, such as black holes, wormholes, and cosmologies, are robust under the extension to $SU(N)$ with an arbitrary number of flavors. We show that key physical features, such as the thermodynamic properties, the baryon number of the configurations, and the geometry, are crucially determined by the number of flavors as well as the coupling constants of the theory. In particular: first, we prove that an increase in the matter content favors the local thermal stability of spherically symmetric black holes. Second, for cosmological solutions, we demonstrate analytically that as the hadronic matter content increases, the scale factor grows more slowly due to enhanced gravitational attraction, while the expansion rate (measured by the Hubble parameter) increases as a consequence of the higher energy density. Third, we find that the throat of traversable wormhole solutions can be enlarged by increasing the number of flavors, since the energy density required to keep the throat open does not grow indefinitely but rather saturates at a finite value.

Therefore, we argue that these solutions cannot be attained from weakly coupled initial data by Cauchy evolution and the solutions of the linearized equations around them cannot be interpreted as approximations to nearby non-linear evolutions. We provide further evidence for this picture with non-linear simulations of the Gregory-Laflamme instability. The non-linear simulations terminate through a loss of hyperbolicity of the Tricomi or Keldysh type, depending on the sign of the Gauss-Bonnet coupling.

Abstract: The Relativistic Continuous Matrix Product State (RCMPS) ansatz introduced in arXiv:2102.07741 is a translation-invariant variational ansatz intended to approximate the ground states of gapped one-dimensional relativistic Quantum Field Theories directly in the continuum limit and in infinite volume. The RQP ansatz generalizes constructions used in the Matrix Product State and Continuous Matrix Product State literature. We apply the RQP ansatz to the sine-Gordon model and find good agreement with results from integrability for both the soliton and breather masses. We also apply the RQP ansatz to the $φ^4$ theory, in both its $\mathbb Z_2$-preserving and $\mathbb{Z}_2$-broken phases, and determine the spectrum of particles and solitons. Abstract: We study how mixed-state correlations reorganize across island transitions in Jackiw-Teitelboim gravity coupled to a non-gravitating bath. Using the partial-dimensional-reduction realization of double holography, we compute reflected entropy for adjacent and disjoint radiation regions in the vacuum and in the thermofield-double state of an eternal black hole. Reflected entropy probes the internal partition of the entanglement wedge and therefore resolves structure that is invisible to the ordinary Page curve: its dominant saddle can change even when the entropy saddle of the union remains fixed, and it vanishes at leading order when the entanglement wedge disconnects. We show that the lower-dimensional reflected-island description agrees, to leading order in the dynamical brane fluctuation, with the higher-dimensional defect-extremal-surface picture. The finite-angle comparison further suggests a projection-covariant identification of island and brane endpoints at linear order. The resulting Page-like curves and Markov gaps provide a geometric probe of the reorganization of the purification and of non-Markovian, multipartite correlation structure during island transitions.

Abstract: Estimating a ground-state energy and preparing a ground state both split into a front-end that supplies an approximate state $|χ\rangle$ and a back-end that reads or refines it. We argue, and measure at every step, that the only thing crossing that split is the front-end's spectral weight distribution $p_n=|\langle E_n|χ\rangle|^2$ over the eigenstates of the problem Hamiltonian $H_P$. Everything downstream is priced in it, so the front-end is a swappable module, and we swap it seven times without altering anything to its right. We call the arrangement WARP.
  Four back-ends spend that distribution. A readout (A) samples $g(t)=\langleχ|e^{-iH_Pt}|χ\rangle$ on the device and extracts true eigenvalues by classical signal processing, returning an energy below the variational bound of the state it was handed, from a signal $50\times$ shorter than a Fourier transform needs. Three routes spend the same account on a state instead. An adiabatic schedule (B) has a minimum gap set by the overlap; a discrete amplification (C) is cheaper in depth but must halt at an index the overlap fixes, so it needs a measured value and not a bound; a recursion (D) of depth $\mathcal{O}(3^m)$ raises the question of whether it can be run at all.
  The interactions are the substance. On stretched N$_2$ a sample-based diagonalisation reports a variational bound 79 mHa high, fifty times outside chemical accuracy; reading the state it has already prepared returns $E_0$ to $1.4\times10^{-6}$ Ha, three orders of magnitude inside it, and needs no new front-end and no new circuit. And because the weights price everything, they must be measurable when scarce, so we beat a weak reference against a strong one and recover overlaps four orders of magnitude below the shot-noise floor. We map the operating envelope throughout, and state the limitations.

Abstract: Applications of quantum devices and simulators require an accurate understanding of their dynamics as well as the effects of environmental noise. While some sources of noise can be modeled as Markovian dissipation, coupling to resonators or vibrational modes often produces non-Markovian dynamics as well, making it difficult to characterize the system, particularly for interacting many-body systems. Here, we identify a broad class of exactly solvable many-body quantum systems with both non-Markovian dynamics and Markovian dissipation. We consider a prototypical model of spins coupled to lossy bosonic modes that mediate Ising-type interactions. We exclude a transverse field but allow local spin dissipation that need not commute with the interactions, including dephasing, relaxation, pumping, and even processes that generate coherences. Using an exact stochastic representation, we decouple the many-body dynamics into the evolution of individual spins, yielding exact spin observables and correlations without adiabatically eliminating the bosons. In the Markovian limit, our solutions describe Ising models with correlated dephasing and nonreciprocal interactions. An example of spin squeezing shows that both the achievable squeezing and its optimal preparation time depend strongly on the interplay between non-Markovian dynamics and Markovian dissipation.

Here we identify the condition for ergodicity-breaking in a system under strong drive, and show how to stabilize DF by exact, local engineering. We further show that the Floquet Hamiltonian $H_{eff} = (i/T)\ln{[U(T)]}$ is non-local when ordered by spin-flips, in a non-DF system, while it turns quasi-local under our simple, local DF engineering that keeps the Hamiltonian $H(t)$ still non-integrable at each $t$. This perplexing non-universality is absent in equilibrium, where non-integrability is believed to imply ergodicity and thermalization universally. Our work paves a way to engineering novel ergodicity-broken interacting Floquet phases of matter in various quantum platforms, and also endows DF-assisted quantum error mitigation and state stabilization techniques (see Nature 653, 1027 (2026)) with unprecedented versatility and flexibility.

Abstract: Quantum error-correcting codes on graph geometries beyond regular euclidean lattices have emerged as a promising route toward robust quantum information processing. A major challenge in quantum error correction is the trade-off between favorable properties of codes constructed in high dimensionality and the need for locality in practical implementations on near-term hardware. Sparse intermediate geometries may combine the advantages of both, but how their structure governs quantum information dynamics and code performance remains unclear. Here we show that the spectral dimension $d_s$, defined from the low-energy spectrum of the graph Laplacian, is the universal parameter connecting these properties. Using Floquet random Clifford circuits on two distinct sparse graph architectures with tunable spectral dimension, we find that $d_s$ governs both quantum information spreading and the scaling of the resulting quantum error-correcting code distance. We identify a geometry-induced entanglement phase transition from localized area-law to ergodic volume-law, whose universality is determined by $d_s$ and exhibits long-range percolation criticality. In the ergodic phase, the circuit acts as a CSS code encoder whose code distance is set by the competition between two logical sectors --- the cost of severing the graph and the length of its shortest winding cycle --- which connectivity pushes in opposite directions. The spectral dimension organizes this trade-off, highlighting a maximum at intermediate $d_s$ retaining sparse connectivity and physical realizability.

Abstract: We study symmetries and anomalies of dissipative Majorana chains described by Lindblad dynamics. Using the operator-state mapping, we represent the Lindbladian as a non-Hermitian operator on a doubled Hilbert space. In this formulation, the ket and bra contours admit strong fermion parities and strong translations, while their diagonal combinations define weak symmetries. For even and odd chain lengths, we diagnose anomalies by comparing symmetry algebras with and without weak fermion-parity defects. These algebras retain the lattice precursor of the modulo-$8$ anomaly of the closed Majorana chain and exhibit projective relations between strong fermion parity and weak translation, as well as between weak fermion parity and strong translation, which are intrinsic to open quantum systems. We establish the IR correspondence between the lattice symmetry algebras and those of the doubled Majorana CFT in four spin sectors, identifying chiral fermion parities emanated from the lattice translations. Finally, we develop the lattice bosonization of the dissipative chain. For even length with collective energy dephasing, combining the defect and no-defect sectors and imposing GSO projections gives rise to an Ising Lindbladian with non-invertible Kramers--Wannier symmetries descending from the anomalous strong translation and fermion parity of the Majorana chain. For odd length, constructing mutually commuting ket- and bra-sector spin operators instead yields an Ising Lindbladian with a Kramers--Wannier duality defect. Our results provide a controlled framework for extending lattice anomalies and non-invertible symmetries to open quantum systems.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the construction of unitary $t$-designs in a multi-photon Fock space on programmable linear-optical interferometers. Using the frame potential as the optimization objective, we identify architectures that realize approximate unitary 1- and 2-designs with considerably fewer controllable parameters than required for universal linear optics. For example, we find numerical evidence suggesting that for unitary 1-designs, the required number of programmable parameters scales empirically as $2d$ with the number of modes $d$, instead of $O(d^2)$. We benchmark the resulting ensembles using single-photon transition probabilities and average channel fidelities. Furthermore, as a practical result, we develop a photon-number estimation protocol for multi-photon Fock states that leverages these optimized 1- and 2-designs. Our findings demonstrate that shallow interferometers can accurately reproduce Haar-averaged first moments, while second-moment-sensitive observables provide a considerably stricter test for approximate unitary 2-designs.

Abstract: Quantum Zeno dynamics constrains a continuously monitored system to measurement-selected subspaces. Here we show that the structure of such Zeno subspaces can be governed by a \emph{latent symmetry}, namely a symmetry that is absent from the geometry of the underlying physical network. We consider a minimal circuit-QED network of coupled microwave resonators subject to quantum-nondemolition (QND) monitoring of local photon occupations. Two distinguished resonators are geometrically inequivalent, yet are exactly exchanged by a latent symmetry of the network. Strong monitoring partitions the single-excitation Hilbert space into three quantum-Zeno subspaces, two of which correspond to localization on the distinguished resonators. Latent symmetry exactly exchanges these two Zeno subspaces and enforces identical escape and residence statistics despite their different microscopic environments. In the strong-measurement regime their residence times grow linearly with the measurement strength and diverge in the ideal Zeno limit, where the measurement subspaces become dynamically disconnected. Our results show that latent symmetry can act as a hidden organizing principle of quantum Zeno dynamics, imposing exact relations between measurement-selected subspaces that are geometrically inequivalent in the physical system.

Abstract: We construct exact deterministic circuits for transposing and inverting an unknown qutrit unitary $U\in\mathrm{SU}(3)$ using only forward queries. Two uses within an antisymmetric encoding implement inversion with eight processing queries and return the same catalyst. Reusing the catalyst implements $m$ transpositions or inversions with $3+4m$ or $3+8m$ total queries, respectively. More generally, we prove that exact transposition and inversion of an unknown $U\in\mathrm{SU}(d)$, $d\ge2$, require at least $d+1$ and $d^2-1$ processing queries, respectively, in fixed causal order. These processing bounds hold for any program state prepared independently of the input state using finitely many forward queries, even if that state is consumed during processing. Both our catalytic qutrit algorithms attain their respective bounds. Moreover, exact qutrit transposition with four processing queries requires at least three preparation queries, and our construction attains this bound.

Abstract: Quantum LDPC codes promise to reduce the overhead of fault-tolerant quantum computation, but their practical use requires efficient logical gates, measurements, and access to non-Clifford resources. Here, we develop a complete set of computational primitives for La-cross hypergraph product codes. We further prove that every rank one logical operator admits $D$ pairwise disjoint physical representatives, enabling parallel gauging and a space--time tradeoff for logical measurements. Finally, we construct an addressable surface code interface for injecting externally prepared resource states into selected logical qubits. Together, these results establish La-cross codes as a simple, low-connectivity generalisation of surface code computation, retaining analogous computational primitives while providing a complete Clifford+$T$ instruction set across multiple encoded qubits.

Abstract: Frustrated magnets host rich emergent behavior because competing interactions generate dense manifolds of low-energy states. The Ising Shastry-Sutherland model is a paradigmatic setting, motivated experimentally by Ising-like rare-earth tetraborides such as TmB$_4$ and ErB$_4$, whose field-induced magnetization plateaus have motivated extensive microscopic modeling. Yet how tunable quantum fluctuations reorganize this material-motivated phase diagram remains largely unexplored. Here, we realize a transverse- and longitudinal-field Ising model on the Shastry-Sutherland lattice using a programmable Rydberg-atom array. We map its quantum phase diagram using density matrix renormalization group calculations and probe corresponding signatures experimentally in arrays containing more than 110 atoms. Beyond the established classical phases, the calculations identify two regimes absent in the classical limit and stabilized at finite transverse field: a $2/5$ filling plateau whose finite stability region emerges from a classically degenerate phase boundary and a symmetry-preserving quantum-dimer regime replacing classically selected collinear order. Experiments on finite open arrays reveal signatures consistent with these regimes across three boundary-pinning conditions, with the resulting response maps agreeing qualitatively with the calculated phase diagram. Our results establish programmable Rydberg-atom arrays as tools for exploring frustrated, material-motivated Hamiltonians and motivate searches for new, quantum-driven magnetization plateaus and quantum-dimer behavior in ErB$_4$ under a transverse field or in strain-tuned TmB$_4$.

Abstract: The Markov length, the length scale governing the exponential decay of conditional mutual information (CMI) in a quantum many-body state, has proven useful for characterizing mixed-state phases of matter. Abstract: The indirect-control paradigm offers a scalable approach to quantum computation by restricting external operations to a small subset of qubits, whereas the entire system evolves under its intrinsic many-body Hamiltonian. This framework reduces hardware complexity and limits the noise introduced through control channels. Recently, Anan et al. proposed an implementation of the variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) within this indirect-control architecture. However, the impact and integration of quantum error mitigation techniques in such a setting remain largely unexplored. In particular, zero-noise extrapolation (ZNE), a widely used error mitigation strategy, has not yet been systematically adapted to indirect-control VQE. A key requirement of ZNE is the controlled amplification of noise, which is commonly achieved through circuit-level techniques such as unitary folding. In the indirect-control framework, however, quantum operations are largely governed by continuous time-evolution under the system Hamiltonian, making the construction of physically realizable inverse operations non-trivial. In this study, we investigate the feasibility of implementing ZNE within the indirect-controlVQEframework.We address the challenges associated with unitary folding in the presence of time-evolution operators and, with a minimal relaxation of the indirect-control constraint, we propose a ZNE-based error-mitigation strategy that exploits the symmetry of the one-dimensional XY spin chain.

Abstract: The resolution of spectroscopic measurements is fundamentally limited by the convolution between a system's intrinsic response and the instrument response function, creating an ill-posed inverse problem. While entangled photons can theoretically bypass these limits, low photon flux has hindered their experimental realization. Here, we propose quantum cross-correlation spectroscopy (QCCS), utilizing macroscopic twin beams (signal and idler beams) to physically circumvent this convolution limit in coherent anti-Stokes Raman scattering. Employing the signal beam as a broadband probe, QCCS achieves all-optical deconvolution through a correlation-enhanced sum-frequency generation channel between the convoluted anti-Stokes and idler beams. By subtracting the uncorrelated background using a 500-femtosecond temporal window, we successfully retrieve narrow Raman bands (8 cm^{-1} and 15 cm^{-1}) completely obscured by a 75 cm^{-1} probe bandwidth without numerical deconvolution or prior knowledge. Our results establish macroscopic quantum correlations as a robust resource for high-fidelity spectroscopy, paving the way for ultrashort-pulse-free operando studies under natural, incoherent excitation conditions.

Abstract: Continuous-variable quantum key distribution utilizes advanced digital signal processing techniques to mitigate channel impairments, driving the progress of high-performance quantum secure communications. Specifically, digital heterodyne receivers are commonly implemented using down-conversion algorithms to detect both components simultaneously where the signal is modulated onto an intermediate frequency to avoid low-frequency noise and bypass stringent optical phase-locking requirements. However, a rigorous analysis of the quantum security implications of the digital down-conversion algorithm remains absent. In this paper, we investigate the algorithm process from the perspective of quantum operator evolution, revealing that the frequency folding effect inherent in down-conversion inevitably mixes the image-frequency information into the baseband signal. To address this issue, we propose a novel operator reconstruction detection scheme based on dual-path digital heterodyne receiver. By performing a non-equal weighted combination of the output operators along two optical paths in the digital domain, this scheme perfectly cancels out the image-frequency operator physically. The robust receiver architecture preserves the baseband noise-suppression advantages, inherently achieves perfect mode balancing for asymmetric optical detection losses, and fundamentally closes the image-frequency crosstalk. Practical experiments have validated the feasibility of the proposed architecture and its effective suppression of image-frequency noise, achieving a performance of 11.4 Mbps @ 42 km. This work lays a solid theoretical and algorithmic foundation for future high-performance, highly secure digital continuous-variable quantum key distribution systems.

Abstract: The uncontrolled endpoint channel can conceal how well a quantum process can protect a logical task under time-resolved control. We formulate a fixed-task resource theory of continuous process tensors with physical access interfaces, entrance-erasing free objects, and budgeted free conversions. Complete-record total-variation error measures performance; the calibrated capability is monotone under conversions that preserve a common free baseline. For unknown-state transmission the baseline is exactly $1-d^{-2}$. Two qubit processes with the same endpoint channel have optimal errors $0$ and $p=\sin^2(2gh)$ at the same finite pulse budget in an ideal-transparent system-only window architecture, forcing any endpoint-only predictor to err by at least $p/2$ on one of them. An exactly solvable family shows how the allocation of a fixed interaction phase around a control window sets the attainable error, and a nontrivial perfect echo requires and attains total control action $π$ in that architecture. The separation extends directly to every qubit unitary target at a budget that includes its boundary gate, while unitary tasks in any dimension have baseline $1-d^{-2}$ whenever a budget-feasible free realization gives the depolarizing output. Complete-record implementation bounds quantify coherent and dissipative control imperfections. Continuous-process randomized compilation supplies physical instrument pullbacks; compatible completion conditions extend their action, and certified dynamical bounds yield achievable task-error and capability guarantees. The resulting theory identifies temporal structure and control investment that endpoint characterization alone cannot resolve.

</details>


### [69] [Pulsed control of an entanglement-breaking index: thermal valleys, a certified valve, and its open-close asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13135)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道经过多次自组合后会逐渐失去与参考系统的纠缠,但其动力学行为(特别是在热浴环境中)尚未被充分理解。研究者希望探索在热比特碰撞回路中,通过调控浴极化参数(bath polarisation)能否出现反常的纠缠动力学现象,即在某些温度窗口内纠缠破坏速度反而减慢,以及这种现象是否可被精确控制和认证。这对理解量子信道的纠缠保护机制和开发量子信息处理协议具有重要意义。

Method: 1) 理论框架:定义纠缠破坏指数为信道自组合直到完全破坏与参考系统纠缠所需的次数;建立热比特碰撞回路模型,其轮次与浴极化p成仿射关系;利用不同p值的轮次精确组合特性,通过球算术(ball arithmetic)认证复合信道的纠缠破坏状态。2) 计算搜索:对3300万个随机电路进行门控搜索,寻找具有"热力学谷"特征的电路(即在p的内部窗口内指数低于无限温度值)。3) 精确验证:针对筛选出的六个认证目标,测试不同极化调度方案(静态、中心脉冲、起始脉冲等)对纠缠恢复和破坏的影响;分析脉冲持续时间、相干倾斜、去极化噪声等参数的作用;通过匹配电路位置比较局部速率,量化非对称性的来源。

Result: 1) 发现了热力学谷现象:对比度最高约3e-3,内部最大指数约90。2) 纠缠恢复的精确控制:在参考目标的88轮窗口保持中,向p=0.05的中心偏移可在恰好8轮时恢复纠缠;所有更短的中心脉冲和起始处的8轮脉冲均认证失败。3) 非对称动力学:关闭开关需要21-35轮,是开启(8轮)的2.6-4.4倍;但匹配电路位置的局部速率一致(误差约2%)。4) 非对称性源于距离和响应模式:关闭需消除2.2倍更大的纠缠余量;热轮次叠加到窗口保持时近乎独立作用,而窗口轮次叠加到热保持时呈集体作用。5) 53轮脉冲可认证地优于静态协议;1.2%的辅助比特相干倾斜可在固定布居下打开开关。6) 噪声敏感性:去极化噪声约4ν/n每轮会杀死n轮后读取的大小为ν的信号(静态信号1.3e-4,8轮开关6.5e-6)。

Conclusion: 本研究首次发现并精确表征了热比特碰撞回路中的热力学谷现象和纠缠动力学的强非对称性。纠缠的恢复和破坏遵循不同的物理机制:恢复过程由热脉冲的近独立作用驱动,可在8轮内精确实现;而破坏过程需要窗口轮次的集体作用,耗时更长且需克服更大的纠缠余量。这种非对称性不源于局部速率差异,而是距离和响应模式的本质区别。在纠缠破坏边界附近,对数余量的杠杆呈现无参数的线性接近行为。这些发现为理解量子纠缠在开放系统中的动力学提供了新视角,并展示了通过精确控制热浴参数调度来操控纠缠的可能性,但同时揭示了该过程对去极化噪声的极高敏感性(开关信号比静态信号敏感20倍),这对实际应用提出了严峻挑战。

Abstract: The entanglement-breaking index of a channel is the number of self-compositions after which all entanglement with a reference system is destroyed. In a thermal qubit collision loop whose round is affine in the bath polarisation p, some circuits have thermal valleys: on an interior window of p the index falls below its infinite-temperature value. Rounds at different p compose exactly, so every schedule of polarisations defines a composite channel whose entanglement-breaking status can be certified in ball arithmetic. A gated search of 33 million random circuits finds no valley with contrast above about 3e-3, with an interior maximum near index 90, and yields six certified targets. At the reference target, held inside its window for 88 rounds, a centred excursion to p = 0.05 restores entanglement at exactly eight rounds: every shorter centred pulse certifiably fails, and so do the same eight rounds at the start. A 53-round pulse certifiably beats the static protocol, a depth-three valley is walked down its staircase by pulse duration, and a 1.2 per cent coherent tilt of the ancilla opens the switch at fixed population. Closing the switch costs 21 to 35 rounds. The asymmetry is not one of local rates, which agree to within about two per cent at matched circuit positions (certified). It is one of distance and response: closing must remove an entanglement margin 2.2 times larger, and while hot rounds added to a window hold act almost independently, window rounds added to a hot hold act collectively. Near the crossing, the lever of the log-margin is that of a linear approach to the entanglement-breaking boundary, with no free parameter. At the reference target, depolarising noise of about $4ν/n$ per round kills a signature of size $ν$ read after $n$ rounds: 1.3e-4 for the static signal, 6.5e-6 for the eight-round switch.

Abstract: Nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond are promising quantum sensors for AC magnetic fields. However, conventional dynamical-decoupling-based magnetometry becomes technically demanding at high frequencies because of the requirement for increasingly short and high-power microwave pulses. This paper describes an AC magnetometry technique based on continuous-wave optically detected magnetic resonance (CW-ODMR) measurements, which enables the detection of signals in the hundred-MHz range. By applying a strong static magnetic field transverse to the NV axis, the energy separation between the relevant electron-spin states was tuned and resonantly coupled with the target radiofrequency (RF) magnetic field, thereby forming RF-dressed states. Unlike pulsed dynamical-decoupling schemes, this approach does not require high-power microwave pulse sequences. The key feature of our method is that the detectable frequency can be tuned by controlling the bias magnetic field strength. Using this methodology, we have experimentally achieved the frequency-tunable detection of AC magnetic fields from 14.4-632 MHz. The sensitivity was 0.558 uT/Hz^(0.5) at 14.4 MHz and 24.4 uT/Hz^(0.5) at 632 MHz. We characterized the field-dependent ODMR contrast and linewidth, and found that their degradation at large transverse fields is a important factor limiting the high-frequency sensitivity. These results establish a simple CW-ODMR approach for widely tunable RF magnetic-field sensing without high-power pulsed spin control.

Abstract: Generative machine learning has shown remarkable results across a wide range of interdisciplinary tasks, motivating significant efforts toward Quantum Generative Models as a path to beyond-classical performance, given their capability of generating classically intractable distributions. However, comparatively little attention has been paid to securely delegating such computations to a powerful remote quantum server. In this work, we propose a blind and verifiable protocol for sampling that can be used for the inference phase of such quantum models. Such a protocol inherits blindness from Universal Blind Quantum Computing and achieves verifiability by exploiting known approaches based on trapification schemes. Alongside, to delegate the training phase, we discuss an extension of the previously proposed verifiable end-to-end Delegated Variational Quantum Algorithms (Inajetovic et al., Phys. Rev. Research, 2026).
  Beyond the usual Accept/Abort outcome characterizing verification protocols, the sampling protocol for inference also provides, even against a malicious server, an estimation of the amount of corrupted generated samples, i.e., a fine-grained feature describing the quality of the outcome connecting verification protocols and application-level benchmarking.

Abstract: We present an explicit quantum circuit that constructs a block encoding of a generator-representable semiseparable (one-pair) matrix directly from its two generators. The building blocks of the circuit are cyclic shifts, swaps, and Hadamard gates, each admitting an efficient implementation through the binary decomposition of the relevant indices.

Abstract: We study how a neural network reorganizes its learned function as training proceeds and as previously unseen parity data accumulate. The trained model is itself a Boolean function on binary strings of finite length. We then evaluate it on all data, inside and outside the training set, and read off its complete sign table. Its $\pm1$ entries are the amplitudes of a normalized state, dubbed the function-state, whose inverse subsystem purity $K_f=1/\textrm{Tr}(ρ_A^2)$ is an effective Schmidt rank. We find that small data additions broaden the Schmidt spectrum, whereas larger additions reconcentrate it in the target mode. The former is called differentiation because the model distinguishes inputs more sharply. The latter is called target-selective recoherence, where the model settles on the single rule shared by the added data. As the dataset grows, the connectivity of the constraint graph defined by the training set increases monotonically, whereas the learned inverse purity changes nonmonotonically. On a fixed connected dataset, as learning proceeds, the Schmidt spectrum begins to reorganize, followed by gains in target alignment and later by improvement in predictive accuracy. This ordering distinguishes three aspects of learning: constraint identifiability, function-state organization, and predictive accuracy. At fixed data size, a function that has already been learned can re-differentiate, an event we call retreat, without changing graph connectivity. Recovery reconcentrates the Schmidt spectrum in a similar target-selective pattern, without exact recurrence of the Boolean state. Thus the percolation of data constraints fixes the identifiable support, while the reduced state produced by training continues to reorganize even after that support stops changing.

</details>

Abstract: The Zak phase provides a fundamental geometric characterization of one-dimensional band structures and, when quantized by symmetry, serves as a bulk indicator of polarization and boundary phenomena. Conventionally, its exact quantization is tied to a global symmetry of the Bloch Hamiltonian, such as inversion or reflection symmetry. Here, we demonstrate a distinct mechanism by which the Zak phase remains exactly quantized even when the corresponding protecting symmetry is explicitly broken at the level of the full Hamiltonian. The mechanism is based on local support symmetry and destructive interference: symmetry-breaking degrees of freedom are coupled to a topological parent system in such a way that the relevant Bloch states cannot leak into them. The preserved states therefore retain their parent wave functions and inherit their quantized Zak phase exactly, despite the absence of the protecting global symmetry. We establish this mechanism for general one-dimensional insulators and demonstrate it in an inversion-broken minimal model and a reflection-broken armchair nanoribbon, where a quantized $π$ Zak phase and associated boundary-localized states persist. We further show that perturbations violating the exact destructive-interference condition continuously dequantize the Zak phase, while a finite regime with a near-$π$ Zak phase and boundary response can survive. Our results establish wave-function support, rather than global symmetry alone, as a mechanism for protecting quantized quantum geometry in one-dimensional systems.

Abstract: Quantum reservoir computing and its special case, quantum extreme learning, are powerful tools for predicting classical discrete time-series with quantum devices. Here, we experimentally generate four continuous time-series signals from a chaotic electronic circuit for forecasting with quantum extreme learning. Since simplified circuit models do not fully capture real behaviour, it is unclear whether algorithms that predict simulated time-series also work for experimental signals. We address discretization via two approaches: (i) sampling local peaks/troughs; and (ii) uniform sampling at the autocorrelation timescale. The former captures resonant behaviour, while the latter fully captures chaotic features as well as allowing the reconstruction of chaotic attractors. Our algorithm achieves high forecasting accuracy for both simulated and experimental signals, demonstrating its power for continuous signal processing on near-term quantum simulators, and remains robust against moderate decoherence.

Abstract: Josephson junctions are the central nonlinear element of superconducting qubits, yet their standard description is reached through a chain of approximations. We propose a purely quantum-mechanical model of the junction, formulated directly in Hilbert space: a charged particle moves on a one-dimensional loop, and the junction is a point at which the wave function obeys self-adjoint boundary conditions, labelled by a coupling strength $λ$, which controls the transparency of the junction, and a phase jump $δ$, which plays the role of the superconducting phase difference. For a loop with a single junction we solve the spectral problem and show that the ground-state momentum expectation, identified with the supercurrent, factorises exactly as $\langle\hat{p}_0\rangle = I_c(δ,λ)\sinδ$. For opaque junctions $I_c$ becomes independent of $δ$ and the first Josephson relation $I=I_c\sinδ$ is recovered; for strongly attractive coupling, where the ground state is bound to the junction, the $\sinδ$ law is also recovered, but with an exponentially suppressed amplitude. For more transparent junctions, however, the model \emph{predicts} deviations from the pure $\sinδ$ law: $I_c$ acquires a dependence on $δ$ already at order $λ^{-2}$, compatible with the higher Josephson harmonics recently found necessary to describe superconducting qubit spectra, and a fully transparent contact yields a current linear in $δ$. When the phase varies in time, a gauge transformation shows that the voltage drop across the junction is proportional to $\dotδ$, so that the second Josephson relation emerges without being imposed. The boundary-condition approach thus reproduces both Josephson relations from first principles and predicts corrections to them, offering a route towards higher-fidelity models of superconducting qubits.

Combining spectral truncation with finite-depth quantum-circuit approximation, invoking the Solovay--Kitaev theorem, we establish quantitative rates that separate the spectral truncation error from the circuit approximation error.

Abstract: A useful thermodynamic model predicts what a device will do without tracking its full microscopic state. We then compare direct measurements of dynamical observables with earlier measurements of the same coarse record, selecting the representation together with its measurement cost. A Bose--Hubbard example yields a compact response table tested over a continuous operating region and on unseen microscopic trajectories. A driven Ising chain shows why history is not always the cheaper choice. The result is a way to turn detailed quantum dynamics into a small, reusable, and explicitly qualified thermodynamic description.

Abstract: The recently developed concept of germ graphs (P. Kurasov {\it et al.}, Phys. Rev. The existing isoscattering non-isospectral graphs contained a two-loop graph labelled $Γ_{A2}$ and a three-loop graph labelled $Γ_{A3}$. These graphs have an equal total metric length, but different topologies. The resulting graphs $Γ_3$ and $Γ_4$ are verified as non-isospectral using their Euler characteristics, while their isoscattering properties are investigated by calculating the one-port scattering matrices numerically.

Abstract: We study correlation-based entanglement evaluation in rank-2 mixed states of two- and multi-qubit systems. The relevant two-dimensional subspace is treated as a logical qubit embedded in the physical Hilbert space, and factorizable realizations of its logical Pauli operators are classified. For two qubits, this yields nontrivial canonical encodings related by local Clifford transformations. The relation between logical and physical Bloch vectors admits a geometric interpretation as a transversely deformed Bloch sphere, leading to closed-form expressions for two-qubit concurrence and generalized concurrence across $N$-qubit bipartitions. The entanglement scaling factor is obtained directly from complementary-subsystem correlations, without prior calibration of state-preparation parameters. Noisy simulations using the IBM Quantum FakeKingston backend model, with readout-error mitigation and zero-noise extrapolation, confirm the analytical results up to $N=10$ qubits. The protocol therefore enables entanglement estimation from only a small number of measurement settings for this class of rank-2 logical states.

Abstract: Practical fault-tolerant quantum computing requires qubit-efficient quantum error correction (QEC). Clifford deformations (CDs) modify the stabilizers of a QEC code to lower its logical error rate at no additional resource cost. However, the number of possible CDs grows exponentially with the number of qubits, making exhaustive search infeasible beyond small codes. In this work, we further show that under circuit level noise the optimal CD depends strongly on both the noise model and decoder. To make systematic CD optimization possible, we introduce Evolution-Based Best-arm Identification (EBBI), a shot-efficient search algorithm that treats the circuit, noise model and decoder as a black box. We benchmark EBBI's performance on the rotated surface code, under two strongly $Z$-biased circuit-level noise models with the PyMatching decoder. At distance 3, EBBI finds the optimal CD using up to $\sim 1100\times$ fewer shots than random search. At distance 5, when run at the target error rate, the median EBBI run converges to the CDs we believe to be optimal, while random search never converges. Under a $Z$-biased SI1000-like noise model, EBBI discovers a new CD, the B-XZZX code, which we generalized to larger distances. It differs from XZZX on only a fraction $\mathcal{O}(d^{-1})$ of the qubits at code distance $d$, yet its relative advantage grows from $\approx 5\%$ at $d = 3$ to $\approx 12\%$ at $d = 11$. We thus show that the B-XZZX pattern found by EBBI can be systematically scaled to larger code distances. Our work represents a first step towards scalable CD optimization for general QEC codes.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum entanglement is a fundamental resource in quantum information science, commonly consumed to perform tasks like quantum teleportation and quantum key distribution. Entanglement concentration converts partially entangled pure states into nearly perfect Bell pairs. While its efficiency is well understood in the asymptotic limit, practical protocols must operate with finite systems. In this finite regime, and at the low error levels relevant in practice, performance is penalized by corrections that reduce the achievable yield. We investigate whether this fundamental finite-size penalty can be mitigated by an entanglement catalyst: an auxiliary state that aids the transformation but is returned exactly and uncorrupted. Although such a catalyst cannot increase the leading-order asymptotic yield, we show that it fundamentally alters the finite-size scaling. For every bipartite pure state with nonuniform Schmidt coefficients, we exactly determine the optimal second-order correction to the yield and show that catalysis completely overcomes this finite-size penalty.

</details>

Result: 提出的两类贝尔不等式成功解决了不同的实验难题：第一类通过线性扩展关联项数量简化了实验要求；第二类提供了更强的噪声容忍能力。两类不等式均能够实现图态及其测量过程的自测试功能。

Conclusion: 本研究为实验检测真正多体非定域性提供了两种互补的贝尔不等式方案，分别针对不同的实验场景：资源受限时可使用线性扩展的第一类不等式，需要高噪声鲁棒性时可使用第二类不等式。两者都支持图态的自测试，为多体量子纠缠的实验验证提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Experimental detection of multipartite Bell nonlocality faces many challenges. The increase in the size of the system demands a higher fidelity of state preparation, a higher detection efficiency, and a larger number of correlators that need to be estimated. These issues are even more pronounced if one wishes to detect a more restrictive variant of Bell nonlocality called genuine multipartite nonlocality (GMNL). In this work, we introduce two classes of multipartite Bell inequalities that are capable of detecting GMNL correlations and address different experimental hurdles in GMNL detection. The first class consists of the number of correlators that scales linearly with the number of parties. The second class increases the number of correlators and, in return, provides higher noise robustness of GMNL detection. Both classes of inequalities are constructed to detect GMNL in graph states, and in fact both can be used for self-testing of graph states and the performed measurements.

</details>

Motivation: 近期量子计算机面临关键限制:每增加一个量子比特虽然能使状态空间加倍,但同时也会增加电路规模、约束开销和硬件噪声暴露。量子比特数量成为实际应用的核心瓶颈。因此需要开发一种方法来减少将优化问题映射到量子硬件时所需的量子比特数量,同时保持问题的完整性。

Result: 在双链路故障延迟弹性路由基准问题上,约简后的问题表示大幅降低了量子比特数量和编译电路复杂度。在相同的囚禁离子量子计算机上运行约简模型,与基准方法相比,成功概率获得了100倍的提升。这一显著改进证明了该方法在实际量子硬件上的有效性。

Conclusion: 精心设计的问题表示方法能够使当前量子硬件在实际优化应用中的效能得到实质性提升。通过结构感知的编码约简,可以在保持优化任务完整性的同时,显著减少量子资源需求和硬件噪声影响,为近期量子计算机解决实际物流和路由优化问题提供了一条可行路径。该工作强调了问题建模在量子计算实用化过程中的关键作用。

Abstract: Qubits are powerful computational resources because each additional qubit doubles the size of the accessible state space. This expressive power comes at a cost: adding qubits also increases circuit size, constraint overhead, and exposure to hardware noise. These limitations are especially critical for near-term quantum computers, making the number of qubits required to formulate a problem a central practical concern. In this work, we present a method for efficiently mapping logistics and routing problems onto qubits through structure-aware encoding reduction. We demonstrate the method on a benchmark problem of latency-resilient routing under dual-link failures. For this benchmark, the reduced formulation substantially lowers both the qubit count and the compiled circuit complexity while preserving the original optimization task. By running the reduced model on the same trapped-ion quantum computer as the benchmark, we obtain a 100X improvement in the success probability. This finding shows how careful problem formulation can make current quantum hardware substantially more effective for practical optimization.

Result: 证明了m阶Jordan块会产生离散的多项式-指数项，其多项式的最高阶数为m-1。这意味着例外通道的动力学特征可以通过测量概率随通道应用次数n的变化规律来识别。建立了Jordan块阶数与可观测动力学行为之间的直接对应关系，使得例外点的存在和性质可以通过实验中重复应用量子操作并测量相应概率来验证。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) are usually introduced as defective degeneracies of non-Hermitian Hamiltonians and, in fully open quantum dynamics, of Liouvillian generators. Here we formulate an operational theory of exceptional points that does not invoke a Hamiltonian or a master equation. The primitive dynamical object is a completely positive trace-preserving (CPTP) quantum channel $Φ$, applied an integer number $n$ of times, while physical data are probabilities $p_n^{\mathbf A}(x|ρ)$ associated with states $ρ$ and observables $\mathbf A$ having measurements results $x$. We define exceptional channels through nontrivial Jordan chains of $Φ$ and show that a Jordan block of order $m$ generates discrete polynomial-exponential terms with maximal degree $m-1$. This places the physics of exceptional points in contact with what can be directly measured in experiments.

</details>

Abstract: Can quantum data reliably answer questions chosen from earlier results? We prove that $M$ adaptive Pauli predictions on $n$ qubits require $Ω(\sqrt{\min\{n,M\}})$ copies at fixed accuracy. Published upper bounds make this tight for $M\le n$. Thus $n$ adaptive predictions cost $Θ(\sqrt n)$ copies, whereas a fixed list costs only $O(\log n)$. The lower bound uses diagonal states and commuting observables, identifying a statistical cost of adaptation. We also quantify how this cost increases with precision.

</details>

Abstract: Rare-earth-ion based molecular crystals are emerging as a promising platform for quantum information applications, combining long optical coherence times with the chemical tunability of coordination chemistry. Here we present a comprehensive optical characterisation of twelve Eu$^{3+}$-based molecular complexes, with a focus on the coherent $^{5}\text{D}_{0} \longrightarrow \ ^{7}\text{F}_{0}$ transition at $\sim$580 nm. Judd-Ofelt analyses are performed across all complexes, providing insights into ligand-field environments, local point-group symmetries, and the resulting branching ratios of the coherent transition. We find that lower point-group symmetry (C$_1$, C$_2$) yields higher branching ratios of the $^{5}\text{D}_{0} \longrightarrow \ ^{7}\text{F}_{0}$ transition, exceeding 1\% in several complexes. Optical inhomogeneous linewidths measured at 4.2 K reveal narrow linewidths for some complexes of below ten GHz, indicative of a highly ordered crystalline environment. Optical coherence measurements of one of the complexes, 5-Eu, yields an optical coherence time of $T_{\text{2}}^{\text{opt}} = 2.3~μ\mathrm{s}$ at 4.2 K, comparable to the best-performing molecular systems reported to date. Systematic characterisation and quantitative comparison will be key to find structure-property relations guiding rational design of optimised complexes for quantum information applications.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 实验结果显示数据集依赖的预测行为,表明增加量子架构复杂度并不普遍提升分类性能。在SPID数据集上,QTN-RQNN将RQNN的F1分数从0.3077提升至0.4516,ROC-AUC从0.4485提升至0.6970。其他数据集表现出不同的性能-资源权衡特性,对循环时间步和电路深度具有不同敏感性。在所有数据集上,相比RQNN,QTN-RQNN每样本的双量子比特门数量从32减少到24,实现25%的减少且不增加四量子比特需求;QTN-QNN相对QNN获得相同的相对减少量。

Abstract: Resource constraints and imperfect quantum operations remain major challenges for quantum machine learning (QML) on noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices, particularly for fault-diagnosis tasks involving imbalanced and heterogeneous data. This work proposes QTN-RQNN, a Quantum Tensor Network-Based Recurrent Quantum Neural Network that integrates tensor-network-inspired nearest-neighbor interactions with recurrent quantum processing. The model sequentially encodes input features while propagating quantum memory across recurrent steps and incorporates QSMOTE for class balancing. QTN-RQNN is evaluated on four heterogeneous fault-diagnosis datasets SPID, CWRUBD, EFDD, and SFDD and compared with QNN, QTN-QNN, and RQNN baselines through classification, ablation, multi-seed, convergence, and quantum-resource analyses. Results show dataset-dependent predictive behavior, indicating that increased quantum architectural complexity does not universally improve classification. On SPID, QTN-RQNN improves the RQNN F1-score from 0.3077 to 0.4516 and ROC-AUC from 0.4485 to 0.6970, while the remaining datasets exhibit different performance-resource trade-offs and sensitivity to recurrent timesteps and circuit depth. Across all datasets, QTN-RQNN reduces the two-qubit-gate count from 32 to 24 per sample relative to RQNN, corresponding to a 25% reduction without increasing the four-qubit requirement; the same relative reduction is obtained for QTN-QNN over QNN. These findings establish QTN-RQNN as a resource-conscious recurrent quantum architecture and highlight the need to jointly consider predictive performance and circuit-resource requirements for near-term quantum fault diagnosis.

</details>

### [94] [Locality-Preparation Time Tradeoffs in Dissipative Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13784)
*Philipp Westhoff,Sara Murciano,Mattia Moroder*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对二次费米子系统建立了耗散算符局域性与态制备时间之间的精确关系，并提出了优化耗散动力学局域性的构造方法。研究表明在临界区，局域化跃迁算符会导致制备时间随系统尺寸代数增长；而在有能隙相（包括平庸相和拓扑相）中，指数局域化的耗散可实现有限制备时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耗散态制备将量子多体态工程化为开放系统动力学的稳态，其所需耗散算符的空间范围决定了模拟实现的可行性以及数字实现的连接性和控制复杂度。然而，这种局域性如何定量地约束制备时间在很大程度上仍不清楚。需要明确局域性与制备时间之间的定量关系，以确定快速耗散制备何时与局域控制兼容，以及临界性何时会带来不可避免的动力学代价。

Method: 研究建立了二次费米子系统中局域性与制备时间的精确关系式，并发展了一种构造方法来优化耗散动力学的局域性同时保持目标态不变。将该框架应用于Kitaev链模型，系统分析了临界区和有能隙区的局域性-制备时间权衡关系。通过分析跃迁算符的空间衰减特性（代数衰减或指数衰减）及其对制备时间标度行为的影响，揭示不同量子相中的动力学约束。

Result: 在Kitaev链模型中发现了截然不同的局域性-制备时间权衡特征：（1）在临界点，抑制跃迁算符的代数拖尾必然导致制备时间随系统尺寸代数增长；（2）在平庸相和拓扑有能隙相中，指数局域化的耗散均可在热力学极限下实现有限的制备时间。这表明有能隙系统允许同时实现局域控制和快速制备，而临界系统则面临本质性的动力学成本。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了耗散态制备中局域性与时间成本之间的定量关系，明确指出快速耗散制备与局域控制的兼容条件取决于系统所处的量子相。临界性会带来不可避免的动力学代价——局域化要求必然导致制备时间的代数增长；而有能隙相（无论拓扑与否）则允许通过指数局域化的耗散实现高效制备。这些结果为设计实际量子态制备协议提供了基本理论指导，揭示了量子相变临界点对耗散动力学效率的本质约束。

Abstract: Dissipative state preparation engineers quantum many-body states as steady states of an open-system dynamics. The spatial range of the required dissipation sets both the feasibility of analog implementations and the connectivity and control complexity of digital ones. Yet how this locality quantitatively constrains preparation times remains largely unknown. Here, we establish an exact locality-preparation time relation for quadratic fermionic systems and develop a construction that optimizes the locality of the dissipative dynamics while preserving the target state. Applied to the Kitaev chain, our framework reveals sharply different locality-preparation time tradeoffs in critical and gapped regimes. At criticality, suppressing the algebraic tails of the jump operators necessarily forces the preparation time to grow algebraically with system size. By contrast, both trivial and topological gapped phases admit exponentially localized dissipation with a finite preparation time in the thermodynamic limit. Our results identify when fast dissipative preparation is compatible with local control and when criticality imposes an unavoidable dynamical cost.

</details>

*Jingyan Xu,Yuanhong Wu,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Sean Patten,Christina Schweikert,D. Frank Hsu*

TL;DR: 本文提出利用组合融合分析(CFA)框架融合多个架构多样化的量子电路和经典模型,通过多样性感知的模型选择和组合策略,在四个二分类基准数据集上均优于单一最佳模型,准确率最高提升6.03%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子电路(VQC)对电路架构高度敏感,但现有量子机器学习流程通常仅选择单一最佳设计而丢弃其他候选架构。本文探索能否利用量子电路搜索空间中的结构多样性,通过模型融合产生更强的预测性能,避免浪费架构搜索过程中产生的多样化模型。

Method: 采用组合融合分析(CFA)框架对29个架构多样化的经典模型和量子电路进行融合。该方法使用排名-分数函数(RSF)和认知多样性(CD)进行模型选择,通过多样性感知选择方法识别最大多样性的模型子集,并采用分数组合和排名组合两种策略进行模型融合,构建经典-量子混合融合系统。

Result: 在四个不同领域的二分类基准数据集上进行实验,基于CFA的混合经典-量子融合方法在所有数据集上均持续优于单一最佳模型,准确率提升最高达6.03%。与相关文献对比,CFA框架在全部四个数据集上均取得更优性能。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了量子电路架构搜索过程中产生的结构多样性可以通过模型融合有效利用,基于多样性感知的CFA框架能够系统性地选择和组合异构的经典-量子模型,在多个领域的分类任务中实现显著且一致的性能提升,为量子机器学习中的模型融合提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Variational quantum circuits (VQCs) are highly sensitive to circuit architecture, yet most quantum machine learning pipelines select a single best design and discard the remaining candidates. This paper addresses whether the structural diversity across quantum circuit search spaces can be used to produce stronger predictions through model fusion. We apply Combinatorial Fusion Analysis (CFA), a model fusion framework that selects and combines multiple scoring systems using rank-score function (RSF) and cognitive diversity (CD), to a pool of 29 architecturally diverse classical models and quantum circuits. Using a diversity-aware model selection method, we identify subsets of maximally diverse models and combine them with both score and rank combination. Experiments on four binary classification benchmarks covering different domains demonstrate that CFA-based hybrid classical-quantum fusion consistently outperform the best individual model on every dataset considered with improvements up to 6.03% in accuracy. When comparing with relevant literatures, the CFA framework further achieves superior performance across all four datasets.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 1) 引入硬件原生的混合qudit-振荡器指令集,适用于d能级内部寄存器与玻色模式的耦合系统;2) 证明三个原生操作族足以实现单个qudit与多个模式耦合的通用性;3) 通过添加离子间纠缠操作扩展到多qudit寄存器的通用性;4) 架构原生支持相空间和边带指令集;5) 在开源框架HybridLane中实现该架构;6) 采用40Ca+离子作为qudit处理器进行实验验证。

Result: 在三色团复合物的耗散振动动力学模拟应用中,采用单个qudit存储整个电子流形替代原有的一量子比特一状态寄存器方案。结果显示:1) 所有电子耦合转化为局域的离子内跃迁;2) 实验室坐标系哈密顿量可直接编译到指令集架构;3) 在40Ca+ qudit处理器的顺序时序模型下,单个Trotter步的运行速度比原方案快约10倍;4) 与已发表参考结果相比,平均均方根误差约为1.1%。

Conclusion: 本文成功展示了基于qudit的离子阱量子处理器架构在提升计算效率方面的显著优势。通过利用离子的多能级结构,该方案无需增加离子数量即可扩展计算空间,使电子耦合局域化为离子内跃迁,实现了哈密顿量的直接编译和约10倍的速度提升,同时保持了高精度(RMSE约1.1%)。这为离子阱量子计算的可扩展性提供了一条有前景的技术路径。

Abstract: Scaling trapped-ion processors by adding ions remains challenging. A complementary route exploits the multilevel internal structure of each ion to increase the computational Hilbert space per ion. We introduce a hardware-native hybrid qudit-oscillator instruction set for a general d-level internal register coupled to bosonic modes and prove that three of its native operation families suffice for universality of one qudit coupled to several modes. Adding an entangling operation between ions extends universality to a connected register of several qudits. The architecture natively supports both phase-space and sideband instruction sets and is implemented in the open-source framework HybridLane. As a representative application, we reproduce the dissipative vibronic dynamics of a three-chromophore complex previously simulated on a hybrid qubit-oscillator superconducting platform. Moving from a one-qubit-per-state register to a single qudit stores the entire electronic manifold in one ion, so every electronic coupling becomes a local intra-ion transition and the lab-frame Hamiltonian compiles directly into the ISA. Under the stated sequential timing model from a 40Ca+ qudit processor, one Trotter step is projected to run about ten times faster on the qudit, with an average RMSE of approximately 1.1 percent against the published reference.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为"truss gadget"的精确构造方法,通过仅使用单体X和Z场以及双体ZZ相互作用来生成双体XX相互作用,克服了量子退火设备中XX相互作用原生不可用的限制。该方法在离子阱量子处理器上得到验证,并在超导量子退火器上应用于加权最大独立集问题,使找到接近基态解的概率提高了约一千倍。
